Implementation details:

- Fast autocorr:
```
def autocorr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size=2*n-1
    fs=1<<(size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, fs)[:size]
```

def smooth_adam(a0, time_limit, tau_factor=0.25, tau_min=1e-8, lr=0.7, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, restart_every=1000, seed=1, shift=0):
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    n=len(a)
    # compute c, base= max or some quantile
    c=poly_conv(a)
    M=c.max()
    tau=M*tau_factor
    tau_end=max(M*1e-5, 1e-12)
    z=np.log(a+eps) # if a has zeros. Use stable log.
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); bc...
    best_score=2*n*M
    for it in range(...): time check
       a=exp(z - logsumexp(z)) # if z from log a, softmax; but if we update z with gradient? Need decide.
```

Coordinate/peak polish:
We can implement a **deterministic greedy mass move** that uses derivatives exactly.
At current a, c, M. For each active peak k (top K), we want reduce c_k. The derivative direction reducing c_k is d = -a_shifted_k, where `a_shifted_k[j] = a_{k-j}` valid, and to keep sum zero we can subtract a small fraction from weighted average? A natural update: Move mass from variables that contribute most to peak (source j and complement k-j) to variables with low coupling to all peaks. The previous coordinate heuristic does. We can improve by solving a small **transfer problem**: For a peak k, contributions are pairs (i,j) with i+j=k. We can choose source i (and complement j) to decrease by δ, and destination d to increase by δ. Change in c_k first order: -2 a_i a_j / a_i? Wait if decrease a_i by δ and increase a_d by δ, sum maintained. Change c_k ≈ -2 a_{k-i}δ + 2 a_d? Actually c_k depends on a_i and a_k-i. If we decrease a_i, complement a_k-i unchanged; derivative c_k wrt a_i =2a_k-i. So δc_k ≈ -2a_{k-i}δ (plus second). Increase a_d: δc_k += 2a_{k-d}δ (if d participates). To reduce peak, choose source among contributing pairs with high a_{k-i}, destination d with low interactions with all active peaks. This is previous. We can make it a line search with exact objective.

For a transfer i->j amount δa, we can compute exact new c quickly? c_new = c + δ*(a_{j?}?) - δ*(?) + δ^2... Let's derive. If a_i -=δ, a_l +=δ (l=dest). The convolution change:
`c_new - c = -δ * (a_i_shifted?) + δ * (a_l_shifted?) + δ^2 e_i e_l`? Since c = 0.5? Actually full convolution c_k = ∑ a_p a_q with p+q=k. Change due to subtract δ from i: for each q, c_q? It corresponds to convolving perturbation d with a and a? Let d has +δ at l, -δ at i. Then δc = d*a + a*d + d*d. Since convolution commutative, δc = 2 d*a + d*d. We can compute `d*a` for one l and one i via shifts (O(n) each) or FFT (O(n log n)). For a single sign change, exact c_new can be updated in O(n) by adding two shifted arrays. This is promising! We can do a fast **exact perturbation local search**:
- State a, c (full length 2n-1), M.
- For a move i->l amount δ: d = -δ e_i + δ e_l. First-order change δc = 2δ (shift_l(a) - shift_i(a)) where shift_j(a) is array with at k: a_{j +? actually c = a*a. The linear term for adding δ at position l: δ * (a shifted such that coeff k = a_{k-l} if valid) plus δ*(a shifted for i -?). More simply, if we add vector b to a, new c = c + b*a + a*b + b*b. For b=δ(e_l - e_i), b*a = δ (a conv e_l - a conv e_i) = δ (a shifted by l - a shifted by i) where (a conv e_l)[k]=a_{k-l}. Since a*b same (commutative). d*d=δ^2(e_l*e_l - e_l*e_i - e_i*e_l + e_i*e_i). The cross term e_l*e_i at lag l+i coefficient -2δ^2.
We can compute delta array length 2n-1:
```
delta_k = 2*δ*(A_shift_l[k] - A_shift_i[k]) + δ^2*( I(k==l) + I(k==i) -2 I(k==i+l) )? Wait b*b = δ^2(e_l - e_i)*(e_l - e_i)=δ^2 e_l*e_l + e_i*e_i -2 e_l*e_i.
Convolution e_j*e_m has 1 at k=j+m.
So delta = 2δ( a_shift(l) - a_shift(i) ) + δ^2( e_l + e_i - 2 e_{i+l}).
```
Where a_shift(l)[k]=a_{k-l} if 0<=k-l<n else 0. (This is a conv e_l). Since a,b real. This is exact.

For each trial: `c_trial = c + delta; M_trial = max(c_trial)`. That's O(2n) ~10k. Python vector ops fast. 1 million trials ~10s maybe. With candidate generation, good.

- `conv_gradient(p, a)`: returns grad wrt a length n as 2*inv. Use p full length 2n-1. Implementation with FFT size nextpow2(2n-1). Need handle n=1.
```
def weighted_grad(p, a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1
    F=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    a_flip=a[::-1].copy()
    Pa=np.fft.rfft(a_flip,F)
    Pp=np.fft.rfft(p,F)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Pa*Pp,F)[:size]
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
But p length size. If p only selected, zero-padded to F. Good.

`def exact_move_delta(c, a, i, l, delta):`
We need compute change array length 2n-1. We can update a copy? For trial, compute `delta_arr = np.zeros_like(c)`? Allocating 10k per trial okay but many; can be costly. We can compute candidate max without full allocation perhaps using in-place temporary? Use a preallocated delta array. But in Python function, allocate. For performance, implement inline in local search with preallocated `tmp`? Since no closures, function can accept tmp.
Pseudo:
```
def apply_move_trial(c, a, i, l, delta):
    n=a.size; size=2*n-1
    tmp = np.zeros(size) # maybe caller passes
    # linear term: 2δ(a_shift_l - a_shift_i)
    # shift_l: k where 0<=k-l<n: tmp[k] += a[k-l]
    s_l = max(0,l); e_l = min(size, l+n)
    tmp[s_l:e_l] += a[:e_l-s_l]
    s_i = max(0,i); e_i = min(size, i+n)
    tmp[s_i:e_i] -= a[:e_i-s_i]
    tmp[s_l:e_l] *= 2*delta? Wait tmp currently a_shift_l - a_shift_i. Multiply whole by 2δ.
    tmp *= 2*delta
    # second term
    tmp[l] += delta*delta  # e_l*e_l at k=l
    tmp[i] += delta*delta  # e_i*e_i
    # cross at k=i+l subtract 2δ^2
    if i+l < size: tmp[i+l] -= 2*delta*delta
    c_trial = c + tmp; return max(c_trial)
```
But careful with order: If l==i, no move. If i+l equals l? only if i=0; cross l with e? Then second term at i+l = l if i=0, we do tmp[l] += δ^2 -2δ^2 = -δ^2; i term? i=0 also tmp[0]+=δ^2. Let's test moving i=0 to l: a0-=δ, a_l+=δ. Exact change at k=l: terms: line 2δ(a_shift_l[l] - a_shift_0[l]) =2δ(a0 - a_l). second: +δ^2 at l (e_l^2) +δ^2 at 0 -2δ^2 at l (e0 e_l cross) -> net -δ^2 at l. Total 2δ(a0 - a_l) -δ^2. Exact c_l = ∑ a_i a_{l-i}. New a0=a0-δ, a_l=a_l+δ. Terms involving: δ? Let's not. It should be correct.

But in code, if we do tmp linear then second, okay. Need ensure `tmp` not modify c. We can preallocate and set to 0 each trial: `tmp.fill(0)` O(size). Then fill slices. We can avoid full allocation by tracking previous. Good.

Local search algorithm more detailed:
```
def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); c=poly_conv(a); M=c.max(); best=...
    P=32; tau_g=0.01*M
    score = compute_score(a, c, P, tau_g)
    stagn=0
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    while time < limit:
       # choose top candidates
       # ensure finite
       min_score = np.min(score) (but score can be zero). To avoid moving to source? 
       potential = a * np.maximum(score - min_score, 0)
       # source candidates: indices with a>floor and potential>0 top 20
       src_idx = np.argpartition(potential, -20)[-20:] sort desc
       dest_idx = np.argpartition(score, 20)[:20] # low
       # filter dest not equal src? okay
       found=False
       for i in src_idx[:10]:
          ai=a[i]; if ai<=1e-12 continue
          # only move if score[i] > min_score
          for l in dest_idx[:10]:
             if l==i: continue
             if score[i] - score[l] <= 1e-15: continue
             # choose delta based on ratio? maybe fraction = min(0.5, 0.1?)
             for frac in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]:
                 delta = frac*ai
                 Mtrial = trial move; if Mtrial < M - 1e-18: accept; found=True; break
             if found: break
          if found: break
       if found:
          # update score in O(n) or recompute if accepted_count % recompute ==0
          score = update_score? Simpler recompute every 5 accepted; else update via delta? We can update score using same delta logic but for score linear: score = conv(p,a). If p fixed, after c update. Could recompute each accepted, FFT 0.2ms, okay.
          c += tmp; a[i]-=delta; a[l]+=delta
          M = Mtrial (or c.max())
          best update
          stagn=0
          # occasionally recompute c from scratch? maybe every 500
          if accepted_count % 1000 ==0: c=poly_conv(a)
        else:
          stagn += 1
          # try random moves from score candidates
          if stagn < 50:
             # sample i from top 100 potential, l from bottom 100 score, frac small
             ... exact accept if improves
          else:
             # recenter: recompute c (maybe drift), recompute score with new c; if still stuck, perturb best local
             # Try a random multiplicative perturbation: z=log(a)+noise, normalize, c, if better accept; else reset to current? Or set a to best with small transfer? 
             # To escape, apply small random move even if not improving with simulated annealing? We can keep current, not best? Maybe just perturb current a slightly and recompute, if improves later.
```
Need avoid infinite. Since we accept only improvements, current is best local; stagn increments. `make_move_tmp(tmp, a, i, l, delta, size):`
```

if i==l or delta<=0: return False
# linear diff d_lin
sl=l; el=min(size,l+n)
tmp[sl:el] += a[:el-sl]
si=i; ei=min(size,i+n)
tmp[si:ei] -= a[:ei-si]
tmp *= (2.0*delta)
dd=delta*delta
if l < size: tmp[l] += dd
if i < size: tmp[i] += dd
cross = i+l
if cross < size: tmp[cross] -= 2.0*dd
return True
```

Pseudo:
```
def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=1, P=32, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); c=poly_conv(a); M=c.max(); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    size=2*n-1; tmp=zeros(size); c_trial=empty(size)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    accepted=0; stagn=0; p_choice=0
    active_P=P
    p=zeros(size); top?; score=zeros(n)
    recompute_p():
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -active_P)[-active_:]
       p.fill(0); p[idx]=1/len(idx)
       score = weighted_grad(p,a)  # returns 2*
       # maybe score /=2
    recompute_p()
    while time<limit:
       # if drift, recompute c
       # compute potential
       min_s = score.min()
       potential = a * np.maximum(score - min_s, 0)
       # top sources: top 12 potential if >0
       if potential.max() <= eps:
          # random restart
       src = top_indices(potential, 12)
       dst = bottom_indices(score, 12)
       found=False
       # sort src by potential desc, dst by score asc
       for i in src[:8]:
         ai=a[i]
         if ai<=1e-12: continue
         for l in dst[:8]:
           if l==i: continue
           if score[i] <= score[l]: continue
           best_delta=0; best_M=M
           for frac in fracs:
              delta=frac*ai
              make_tmp(tmp,...)
              np.add(c,tmp,out=c_trial); Mt=c_trial.max()
              if Mt < best_M: best_M=Mt; best_delta=delta; best_tmp_store? tmp overwritten
           if best_delta>0:
              # recompute accepted with chosen frac? tmp currently last; need regenerate for best_delta because tmp overwritten in loop.
              make_tmp(tmp,...best_delta)
              np.add(c,tmp,out=c); a[i]-=best_delta; a[l]+=best_delta
              # update score
              update score
              M=best_M (or c.max after)
              found=True; accepted+=1; stagn=0; best update
              if accepted % 20 ==0: recompute score (or p if stagn?)
              break
         if found: break
       if not found:
          # random biased trials
          for _ in range(20): ...
       if not found:
          stagn +=1
          if stagn == 10 or stagn==50:
             recompute_p with maybe P+?
          if stagn > 200:
             # perturb best
             z = np.log(best_a + floor)+ rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
             a=softmax(z); c=poly_conv(a); M=c.max(); recompute_p; stagn=0
             # if M > best_M? continue
       if accepted % 10000 ==0: recompute c from a to fix drift
```

def quick_score_normalized(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); n=a.size; if n==0 return inf
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
    an=a/s; c=poly_conv(an); M=c.max(); return float(2*n*M)
```

Parametric scan function:
```
def parametric_candidates(n):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float); cand=[]
    # uniform
    cand.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # powers p,q
    for p in [...]: for q in [...]: a=(idx+1)**p * (n-idx)**q; ...
    # exp linear/quadratic
    for lam in np.linspace(-3,3,13): a=exp(lam*(x-0.5))
    for lam1 in [-2,0,2]: lam2 in [-3..3]: a=exp(lam1*x + lam2*(x-0.5)**2)
    # sinusoidal
    for freq [1,2,3], phase... a=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2πfreq x+phase)+...
    # beta-like edges: a=(x+eps)**p*(1-x+eps)**q
    return [norm_sum1(a) for valid]
```
Need avoid too many. We can evaluate top score sort.

`lse_weights`:
```
def lse_weights(c, tau, active_count):
    size = c.size
    if active_count <=0 or active_count>=size:
       vals = c - c.max()
       exp = np.exp(vals/tau)
       s=exp.sum()
       if s==0: p=np.zeros(size); p[np.argmax(c)]=1; else p=exp/s
    else:
       k=int(active_count)
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
       vals=c[idx]-c[idx].max()
       exp=np.exp(vals/tau); s=exp.sum(); p=np.zeros(size); p[idx]=exp/s
    return p
```
If tau very small, only max selected exp=1; p spike at top among idx. If active_count large, okay.

def top_lse_weights(c, k, tau):
   k=min(k,c.size)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   vals=c[idx]-c[idx].max()
   exp=np.exp(vals/tau)
   s=exp.sum()
   idx=idx[exp>0]? if sum underflow? at least max.
   return idx, exp/s
```

Implement `resample(a, new_n)` with linspace interpolation, norm.
```
def resample(a, new_n):
    old_n=a.size; if same return a.copy()
    xs=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    b=np.interp(xnew,xs,a); b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-15: b=np.ones(new_n)/new_n; else b/=s
```
Use.

def selected_grad(a, sel, weights):
    n = a.size
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(sel.size):
        k = int(sel[r])
        w = float(weights[r])
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        factor = 2.0 * w
        if k < n:
            m = k + 1
            # coeff a[k], a[k-1], ..., a[0]
            grad[:m] += factor * a[k:-m-1:-1]  # if m=k+1, a[k-1?]. Test k=0: a[0:-2:-1]? bad.
```

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)/max(1,a.size)
    a = a/s
    if not np.isfinite(a).all(): ...
    return a
```

`poly_conv`:
```
def poly_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    F=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,F)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,F)[:size]
    return C
```
If n=0? avoid.

`quick_score`:
```
def quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-300: return inf
    a=a/s; c=poly_conv(a); M=c.max(); if not np.isfinite(M): return inf; return 2.0*n*M
```
Since lower bound ~1, M ~1/n.

`top_lse_weights`:
```
def top_lse_weights(c, k, tau):
    size=c.size
    k=max(1, min(k,size))
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    vals=c[idx]
    vmax=vals.max()
    exp=np.exp((vals-vmax)/tau)
    s=exp.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
       # spike at max among idx
       weights=np.zeros(k); weights[np.argmax(vals)]=1
       return idx, weights
    weights=exp/s
    # optional remove zero weights? keep
    return idx, weights
```
If tau very small and vals-vmax negative large => exp underflow 0; sum may be 1 from max, ok. If multiple max, exp=1. Good.

`selected_grad`: use `sel` 1D array, `weights` same length. Ensure lengths.
```
def selected_grad(a, sel, weights):
    n=a.size
    grad=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(sel.size):
       k=int(sel[r]); w=float(weights[r])
       if w <= 0: continue
       f=2.0*w
       if k < n:
          m=k+1
          grad[:m] += f * a[k::-1]
       else:
          start=k-n+1
          m=n-start
          grad[start:start+m] += f * a[n-1:n-m-1:-1]
    return grad
```
Potential if `a[k::-1]` for k large? k<n so k <= n-1, okay. For branch k>=n, n-m-1 = n-(n-start)-1 = start-1. So coeff a[n-1:start-1:-1]. Could use that: `a[n-1:start-1:-1]`. If start=1, stop=0, includes a[n-1]..a[1]. If start=n-1, stop=n-2, includes a[n-1]. Good. More readable:
```
coeff = a[n-1:start-1:-1]
```
For start=1, stop=0; if n=4, a[3:0:-1] gives a3,a2,a1 length3. Good. For start=n-1, a[n-1:n-2:-1] length1. Use.

`smooth_adam`:
Parameters: `a0, time_limit, seed=1, tau_factor=0.25, tau_decay=0.9997, lr=0.35, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, active_count=96, restart_every=1200, verbose=True, floor_frac=1e-12`.
Detailed code:
```
def smooth_adam(a0, time_limit, ...):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None, inf
    if time_limit <=0: return a, inf
    # add tiny asym? no, a0 may.
    floor = 1e-12 + 1e-12/n? let's set eps=1e-12
    a = a + eps; a = a/a.sum()
    c=poly_conv(a); M=c.max(); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    tau = max(M*tau_factor, 1e-12)
    tau_min = max(M*1e-6, 1e-14)
    z=np.log(a); z -= z.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    eps_step=1e-8
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       t +=1
       # softmax
       zm = z.max()
       if not finite: reset
       ae = np.exp(z - zm)
       ae /= ae.sum()
       c = poly_conv(a)  # use new a? Wait a should be ae. We set a=ae.
       M = c.max()
       if M < best_M - 1e-18: best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0 else stall++
       sel,w = top_lse_weights(c, active_count, tau)
       g = selected_grad(a, sel, w)
       g -= g.mean()
       # optional if not finite: reset
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(g*g)
       bc1=1-beta1**t; bc2=1-beta2**t
       step = lr * (m/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v/bc2)+eps_step)
       z = z - step
       z -= z.mean()
       # if z range too large or nonfinite, recover
       if not np.isfinite(z).all() or (z.max()-z.min() > 100):
          a = best_a + rng.normal(0,0.02,best_a); a=norm_sum1(a); z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean(); m=v=0; stall=0
       tau = max(tau*tau_decay, tau_min)
       if stall > restart_every:
          a = best_a * (1 + rng.normal(0, 0.03, n))
          a = norm_sum1(a); z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean(); m=zeros;v=zeros;stall=0; tau=max(tau, M*0.1)
       if verbose and t%2000==0: print
    return best_a, best_M
```
Need be careful: We use `a=ae` before `poly_conv`. We should set `a=ae`. In code:
```
ae = exp...; ae/=ae.sum(); a=ae
c=poly_conv(a)
```
Good.
`best_M` initialized with c from initial a. After first iter, `stall` update. For `M < best_M`, if equal no.
`beta1**t` for large t okay maybe underflows to 0; bc=1. Could precompute? okay.
We recalc `M` after set best. For tau initial, `M` maybe. If M very small/zero? Sequence sum1 so M>0. If all zeros not.
Time check top of loop; if time_limit small, still one iter? okay.

At restart, use `best_a` maybe with zeros; add eps. `a = best_a + 1e-10 + 0.03*rng.normal? If normal makes negative, norm_sum1 clips. Good. Use `a = best_a*(1+noise); a = a + 1e-12; norm_sum1`.

def local_setup_weights(a,c,P):

`top_k_indices` top-level:
```
def top_k_indices(arr,k):
   k=min(k,arr.size)
   if k<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
   idx=np.argpartition(arr, -k)[-k:]
   # sort descending
   vals=arr[idx]; order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]; return idx[order].astype(int)
```

Now, local_search exact `make_move_delta` formula with second-order at 2i,2l,i+l. Need ensure if two locations same, handle signs correctly.
```
def make_move_delta(tmp, a, i, l, delta, size):
    tmp.fill(0.0)
    if i == l or delta <= 0.0: return
    n=a.size
    sl=l; el=min(size, l+n)
    tmp[sl:el] += a[:el-sl]
    si=i; ei=min(size, i+n)
    tmp[si:ei] -= a[:ei-si]
    tmp *= (2.0*delta)
    dd=delta*delta
    k=2*i
    if k < size: tmp[k] += dd
    k=2*l
    if k < size: tmp[k] += dd
    k=i+l
    if k < size: tmp[k] -= 2.0*dd
```
Test with i=0,l=1 from before: linear diff as before, second at 0 +δ², at2 +δ², at1 -2δ². Good. Test i=l skip, no change. Test moving i=1,l=0 (source1 to dest0): δ positive. Linear diff a_shift0 - a_shift1? We move from i=1 to l=0: d=δe0-δe1, linear 2δ(a_shift0 - a_shift1), second at2,0,1. Good.

`np.add(c, tmp, out=c_trial)` where c_trial same shape. After max, if accept `np.add(c,tmp,out=c)`. Good.

Important: In local greedy, when trying multiple fracs for same pair, we call `make_move_delta` which fills tmp. We keep best_delta but not tmp. After loop, call again. Good. But if best_delta first frac, then later we overwrite tmp, okay.

def local_setup_weights(a, c, P):
    n=a.size
    P = min(max(1,int(P)), c.size)

    p_full = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p_full[idx] = w
    if idx.size:
       score = 0.5 * selected_grad(a, idx, w)
    else: score=np.zeros(n)
    return idx, w, p_full, score
```

Shift testing:
```
def try_shifts(a, n_shifts=32, step=None):
   n=a.size; best=a; best_s=quick_score(a)
   if n_shifts>1:
      step=max(1,n//n_shifts)
      for sh in range(0,n,step):
         if sh==0: continue
         b=np.roll(a, sh)
         s=quick_score(b)
         if s<best_s: best=b; best_s=s
   return best,best_s
```
Rolling is O(n). quick FFT. 32 shifts ~32*0.5ms=16ms. Good. We can run before final evaluate. But rolling may change sum same. Good.

```
def safe_evaluate(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
       if arr.size==0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return inf
       arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
       if arr.sum()<0.01: # evaluator would inf; scale?
           # We can scale up? Objective scale invariant, but evaluator inf if sum<0.01. Our normalized sum1. If tiny zeros, no.
           pass
       seq=[float(x) for x in arr]
       val=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       return val if np.isfinite(val) else inf
    except Exception:
       return inf
```

Parametric candidates count: Need not too many. We'll define:
```
def parametric_candidates(n):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    t = (np.arange(n)+1)/(n+1) maybe
    out=[]
    out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # linear exponentials lam -4..4 step 0.5
    for lam in np.linspace(-3,3,13): out.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(lam*(x-0.5))))
    # quadratic lam1 -3..3, lam2 -4..4
    for l1 in (-2,-1,0,1,2):
       for l2 in (-3,-2,0,2,3):
          a=np.exp(l1*x + l2*(x-0.5)**2); out.append(norm_sum1(a))
    # beta powers p -0.5..2 q -0.5..2
    eps=1e-6
    for p in (-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5,1,2):
       for q in (-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5,1,2):
          a=(x+eps)**p*(1-x+eps)**q
          out.append(norm_sum1(a))
    # sinusoids
    for freq in (1,2,3):
       for phase in (0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2):
          a=0.8+0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x+phase)
          out.append(norm_sum1(np.maximum(a,0.01)))
    # Gaussian humps
    for c in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
       for sig in (0.1,0.2,0.35):
          a=0.3+0.7*np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig))
          out.append(norm_sum1(a))
    return out
```
This ~13+5*5+49+12+9=117. Good.

Maybe some parameters yield very spiky (negative power -> endpoints huge) causing endpoint coefficients high but quick score handles. Norm may have large values >1000? If p negative and eps, a values up to 1/eps^0.5 ~1000, after norm divided by n maybe? max value relative ~? If base big at endpoints, sum not huge? Could values large but quick normalizes; objective okay. But for safe_evaluate, normalized values <1. Good.

Potential issue: `norm_sum1` for `a` with large dynamic range may under/overflow? exp lam 1.5 max 4.5; fine.

   if n <=1 or max_shifts <=1: return best,best_s
   step=max(1, n//max_shifts)
   for sh in range(0,n,step):
      if sh==0: continue

      s=quick_score(b)
      if s < best_s:

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2
using a combination of:
  - parametric initializations,
  - smooth log-domain Adam with reweighted active-set LSE gradients,
  - fast exact single-transfer local search with pandas/NumPy-free buffers.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
        a = a / s
    return a


def resample(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = max(1, a.size)
    new_n = max(1, int(new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(xn, xs, a)
    return norm_sum1(b)


def poly_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if size <= 1:
        return a * a
    F = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)[:size]
    return C


def quick_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = poly_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def safe_evaluate(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return float("inf")
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            # Evaluator rejects too-small sums.  The objective is scale
            # invariant under the evaluator's clipping, so rescale safely.
            a = a * (1000.0 / max(1.0, a.size))
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                return float("inf")
        seq = [float(x) for x in a]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return float("inf")
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def top_lse_weights(c, k, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    size = c.size
    k = max(1, min(int(k), size))
    if k >= size:
        idx = np.arange(size, dtype=int)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:].astype(int)
    vals = c[idx]
    vmax = float(vals.max())
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-12
    expv = np.exp((vals - vmax) / tau)
    s = float(expv.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if idx.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        return idx, w
    w = expv / s
    return idx, w


def selected_grad(a, sel, weights):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if sel.size == 0:
        return grad
    for r in range(sel.size):
        k = int(sel[r])
        w = float(weights[r])
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        f = 2.0 * w
        if k < n:
            m = k + 1
            grad[:m] += f * a[k::-1]
        else:
            start = k - n + 1
            m = n - start
            grad[start:start + m] += f * a[n - 1:start - 1:-1]
    return grad


def smooth_adam(a0, time_limit, seed=1, tau_factor=0.20, tau_decay=0.9997,
                lr=0.35, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, active_count=64,
                restart_every=1200, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, quick_score(a)

    eps_base = 1e-12
    a = a + eps_base
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    c = poly_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    tau = max(M * tau_factor, 1e-12)
    tau_min = max(M * 1e-6, 1e-14)
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= z.mean()

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        t += 1

        zm = z.max()
        if not np.isfinite(zm):
            a = best_a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            z = np.log(a)
            z -= z.mean()
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            stall = 0
            tau = max(tau, best_M * 0.2)
            continue

        e = np.exp(z - zm)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            a = best_a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            continue
        a = e / se

        c = poly_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            a = best_a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            c = poly_conv(a)
            M = float(c.max())

        if M < best_M - 1e-18:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        sel, w = top_lse_weights(c, active_count, tau)
        g = selected_grad(a, sel, w)
        g -= g.mean()

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            a = best_a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            z = np.log(a)
            z -= z.mean()
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            stall = 0
            continue

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)

        bc1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** t
        bc2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** t
        step = lr * (m / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v / bc2) + 1e-8)

        z = z - step
        z -= z.mean()

        zrange = z.max() - z.min()
        if (not np.all(np.isfinite(z))) or zrange > 100.0:
            a = best_a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            z = np.log(a)
            z -= z.mean()
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            stall = 0
            tau = max(tau, best_M * 0.2)

        tau = max(tau * tau_decay, tau_min)

        if stall > restart_every:
            a = best_a * (1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n))
            a = a + eps_base
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            z = np.log(a)
            z -= z.mean()
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            stall = 0
            tau = max(tau, best_M * 0.15)

        if verbose and (t % 2500 == 0):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"    smooth t={t} ({elapsed:.0f}s) n={n} score={2.0*n*best_M:.8f} tau={tau:.3e}")

    return best_a, best_M


def top_k_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    k = max(0, min(int(k), arr.size))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if k >= arr.size:
        return np.argsort(arr)[::-1].astype(int)
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, -k)[-k:].astype(int)
    vals = arr[idx]
    order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    return idx[order]


def bottom_k_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    k = max(0, min(int(k), arr.size))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if k >= arr.size:
        return np.argsort(arr)[:k].astype(int)
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, k)[:k].astype(int)
    vals = arr[idx]
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    return idx[order]


def local_setup_weights(a, c, P):
    n = a.size
    P = max(1, min(int(P), c.size))
    idx = top_k_indices(c, P)
    w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / max(1, idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
    p_full = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx.size:
        p_full[idx] = w
    score = 0.5 * selected_grad(a, idx, w)
    return idx, w, p_full, score


def make_move_delta(tmp, a, i, l, delta, size):
    tmp.fill(0.0)
    if i == l or delta <= 0.0:
        return
    n = a.size

    sl = l
    el = min(size, l + n)
    tmp[sl:el] += a[:el - sl]

    si = i
    ei = min(size, i + n)
    tmp[si:ei] -= a[:ei - si]

    tmp *= 2.0 * delta

    dd = delta * delta
    k = 2 * i
    if k < size:
        tmp[k] += dd
    k = 2 * l
    if k < size:
        tmp[k] += dd
    k = i + l
    if k < size:
        tmp[k] -= 2.0 * dd


def local_search(a0, time_limit, seed=1, P=64, max_pairs=8,
                 fracs=(0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002), verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0 + 1e-12)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, quick_score(a)

    size = 2 * n - 1
    c = poly_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    cur_M = M

    tmp = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    c_trial = np.empty(size, dtype=np.float64)

    P_eff = max(1, min(int(P), 512, size))
    idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    accepted = 0
    stagn = 0
    slow_rounds = 0
    perturbed = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        found = False

        min_s = float(score.min())
        potential = a * np.maximum(score - min_s, 0.0)

        if np.isfinite(potential).any() and float(potential.max()) > 1e-18:
            src = top_k_indices(potential, max_pairs)
            dst = bottom_k_indices(score, max_pairs)

            for ii in range(src.size):
                i = int(src[ii])
                ai = float(a[i])
                if ai <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                si_score = float(score[i])
                for jj in range(dst.size):
                    l = int(dst[jj])
                    if l == i:
                        continue
                    if si_score - float(score[l]) <= 1e-15:
                        continue

                    best_trial_M = cur_M
                    best_delta = 0.0

                    for frac in fracs:
                        delta = ai * float(frac)
                        if delta <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        make_move_delta(tmp, a, i, l, delta, size)
                        np.add(c, tmp, out=c_trial)
                        Mt = float(c_trial.max())
                        if Mt < best_trial_M - 1e-16:
                            best_trial_M = Mt
                            best_delta = delta

                    if best_delta > 0.0:
                        make_move_delta(tmp, a, i, l, best_delta, size)
                        np.add(c, tmp, out=c)
                        a[i] -= best_delta
                        a[l] += best_delta
                        if a[i] < 0.0:
                            a[i] = 0.0

                        m_l = min(n, size - l)
                        if m_l > 0:
                            score[:m_l] += best_delta * p_full[l:l + m_l]
                        m_i = min(n, size - i)
                        if m_i > 0:
                            score[:m_i] -= best_delta * p_full[i:i + m_i]

                        cur_M = float(c.max())
                        found = True
                        accepted += 1
                        stagn = 0
                        slow_rounds = 0

                        if cur_M < best_M - 1e-18:
                            best_M = cur_M
                            best_a = a.copy()

                        if accepted % 2500 == 0:
                            a = a + 1e-12
                            a = norm_sum1(a)
                            c = poly_conv(a)
                            cur_M = float(c.max())
                            if cur_M < best_M - 1e-18:
                                best_M = cur_M
                                best_a = a.copy()
                            idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)

                        if accepted % 60 == 0:
                            idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)

                        break
                if found:
                    break

        if not found:
            # Randomized biased attempts.
            if np.all(np.isfinite(score)) and float(np.max(potential if hasattr(potential, 'max') else a)) > 0.0:
                topcnt = min(120, n, max(8, max_pairs * 12))
                src_rand = top_k_indices(potential, topcnt)
                dst_rand = bottom_k_indices(score, topcnt)

                if src_rand.size > 0 and dst_rand.size > 0:
                    for _ in range(25):
                        i = int(idx_rand := rng.choice(src_rand))
                        l = int(rng.choice(dst_rand))
                        if i == l:
                            continue
                        ai = float(a[i])
                        if ai <= 1e-14:
                            continue
                        if float(score[i]) - float(score[l]) <= 1e-16:
                            continue
                        delta = ai * float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.05))
                        make_move_delta(tmp, a, i, l, delta, size)
                        np.add(c, tmp, out=c_trial)
                        Mt = float(c_trial.max())
                        if Mt < cur_M - 1e-16:
                            np.add(c, tmp, out=c)
                            a[i] -= delta
                            a[l] += delta
                            if a[i] < 0.0:
                                a[i] = 0.0

                            m_l = min(n, size - l)
                            if m_l > 0:
                                score[:m_l] += delta * p_full[l:l + m_l]
                            m_i = min(n, size - i)
                            if m_i > 0:
                                score[:m_i] -= delta * p_full[i:i + m_i]

                            cur_M = float(c.max())
                            accepted += 1
                            stagn = 0
                            slow_rounds = 0
                            if cur_M < best_M - 1e-18:
                                best_M = cur_M
                                best_a = a.copy()
                            found = True
                            break

            if not found:
                stagn += 1
                slow_rounds += 1

                if stagn == 20 or stagn == 80:
                    P_eff = min(P_eff * 2, 512, size)
                    idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)

                if stagn == 120:
                    a = a + 1e-12
                    a = norm_sum1(a)
                    c = poly_conv(a)
                    cur_M = float(c.max())
                    if cur_M < best_M - 1e-18:
                        best_M = cur_M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)
                    stagn = 0
                    slow_rounds = 0

                if slow_rounds > 50:
                    # Escape: perturb the best known point.
                    perturbed += 1
                    z0 = np.log(best_a + 1e-12)
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
                    z = z0 + noise
                    z -= z.mean()
                    e = np.exp(z - z.max())
                    s = float(e.sum())
                    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                        a = norm_sum1(best_a)
                    else:
                        a = e / s
                    a = a + 1e-12
                    a = norm_sum1(a)
                    c = poly_conv(a)
                    cur_M = float(c.max())
                    if cur_M < best_M - 1e-18:
                        best_M = cur_M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    P_eff = max(1, min(P_eff, 256, size))
                    idx, w, p_full, score = local_setup_weights(a, c, P_eff)
                    stagn = 0
                    slow_rounds = 0

        if verbose and (accepted % 4000 == 0 and accepted > 0):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"    local accepted={accepted} ({elapsed:.0f}s) n={n} best={2.0*n*best_M:.8f} stall={stagn}")

    return best_a, best_M


def parametric_candidates(n):
    n = max(16, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = []

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    # Exponential tilts.
    for lam in np.linspace(-3.0, 3.0, 13):
        a = np.exp(lam * (x - 0.5))
        out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    # Quadratic shape.
    for l1 in (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        for l2 in (-3.0, -2.0, 0.0, 2.0, 3.0):
            a = np.exp(l1 * x + l2 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
            out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    # Beta-like edge powers.
    eps = 1e-6
    for p in (-0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for q in (-0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            a = (x + eps) ** p * (1.0 - x + eps) ** q
            out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    # Sinusoidal modulations.
    for freq in (1, 2, 3):
        for phase in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
            a = 0.7 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
            out.append(norm_sum1(np.maximum(a, 0.01)))

    # Gaussian humps.
    for cent in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        for sig in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
            a = 0.25 + 0.75 * np.exp(-((x - cent) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    # Few point clusters (smear them a bit so they remain nontrivial).
    for c1 in (0.2, 0.45, 0.7):
        for w1 in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
            a = w1 * np.exp(-((x - c1) ** 2) / (2.0 * 0.08 ** 2))
            a += (1.0 - w1) * np.exp(-((x - (1.0 - c1)) ** 2) / (2.0 * 0.12 ** 2))
            out.append(norm_sum1(a))

    return out


def try_shifts(a, max_shifts=32):
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, quick_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_s = quick_score(a)
    step = max(1, n // max(1, max_shifts))
    for sh in range(0, n, step):
        if sh == 0:
            continue
        b = np.roll(a, sh)
        s = quick_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best = b
            best_s = s
    return best, best_s


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1800.0
    total = max(120.0, min(total, 1800.0))
    # Reserve final validation/shift time.
    time_budget = total - 55.0

    def remaining_time():
        return time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)

    best_a = None
    best_fast = float("inf")

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum()) > 1e-300:
            parent = norm_sum1(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    base_n = 5200
    if parent is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
        pn = parent.size
        if pn < 512:
            base_n = 512
        elif pn > 12000:
            base_n = 8192
            parent = resample(parent, base_n)
        else:
            base_n = pn
        print(f"Parent valid: n={base_n}")
    else:
        base_n = 4096
        print("No valid parent; using generated initializations.")

    candidates = []
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(parent)

    try:
        cand_list = parametric_candidates(base_n)
        candidates.extend(cand_list)
    except Exception:
        candidates.append(np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n))

    # Try a couple of nearby lengths by resampling the parent (if present).
    if parent is not None:
        for alt_n in (4096, 6144, 8192):
            if alt_n == base_n:
                continue
            if 1024 <= alt_n <= 12000:
                try:
                    candidates.append(resample(parent, alt_n))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    scored = []
    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            if cand.size == 0 or cand.size > 12000:
                continue
            s = quick_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                scored.append((s, cand))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if scored:
        keys = np.array([s for s, _ in scored], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(keys)
        for ii in order[:8]:
            s, cand = scored[int(ii)]
            if s < best_fast:
                best_fast = s
                best_a = cand.copy()

        top_candidates = []
        seen_shapes = set()
        for ii in order[:8]:
            s, cand = scored[int(ii)]
            key = (cand.size,)
            if key in seen_shapes:
                continue
            seen_shapes.add(key)
            top_candidates.append(cand)
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n)
        best_fast = quick_score(best_a)
        top_candidates = [best_a.copy()]

    print(f"Initial best score (fast) = {2.0 * len(best_a) * (best_fast / (2.0 * len(best_a))):.8f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Phase 1: smooth optimize the best candidate.
    rem = remaining_time()
    if rem > 120 and best_a is not None:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.38, 430.0)
        if t1 > 25:
            print(f"\n=== Smooth phase A: n={best_a.size}, {t1:.0f}s ===")
            a_out, _ = smooth_adam(
                best_a,
                t1,
                seed=101,
                tau_factor=0.20,
                tau_decay=0.9997,
                lr=0.35,
                active_count=64,
                restart_every=1200,
                verbose=True,
            )
            if a_out is not None:
                s = quick_score(a_out)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_fast:
                    best_fast = s
                    best_a = a_out.copy()
                    print(f"  SmoothA improved: fast={best_fast:.8f}")

            rem = remaining_time()
            if rem > 45:
                tloc1 = min(rem * 0.22, 130.0)
                if tloc1 > 15:
                    print(f"  Local polish A: {tloc1:.0f}s")
                    a_out, _ = local_search(best_a, tloc1, seed=202, P=64, max_pairs=8, verbose=False)
                    if a_out is not None:
                        s = quick_score(a_out)
                        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_fast:
                            best_fast = s
                            best_a = a_out.copy()

    # Phase 2: smooth an alternative candidate.
    rem = remaining_time()
    alt_done = False
    for alt in top_candidates:
        if alt_done:
            break
        rem = remaining_time()
        if rem < 90:
            break
        if best_a is not None and alt is best_a:
            continue
        if alt.size > 12000:
            continue
        t2 = min(rem * 0.22, 240.0)
        if t2 < 25:
            break
        print(f"\n=== Smooth phase B: n={alt.size}, {t2:.0f}s ===")
        a_out, _ = smooth_adam(
            alt,
            t2,
            seed=303,
            tau_factor=0.30,
            tau_decay=0.9995,
            lr=0.45,
            active_count=128,
            restart_every=1800,
            verbose=True,
        )
        if a_out is not None:
            s = quick_score(a_out)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_fast:
                best_fast = s
                best_a = a_out.copy()
                print(f"  SmoothB improved: fast={best_fast:.8f}")

        rem = remaining_time()
        if rem > 40:
            tloc2 = min(rem * 0.18, 100.0)
            if tloc2 > 15:
                a_out, _ = local_search(a_out, tloc2, seed=404, P=80, max_pairs=10, verbose=False)
                if a_out is not None:
                    s = quick_score(a_out)
                    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_fast:
                        best_fast = s
                        best_a = a_out.copy()
        alt_done = True

    # Phase 3: main local polish on global best.
    rem = remaining_time()
    if rem > 80 and best_a is not None:
        tloc3 = rem - 70.0
        print(f"\n=== Main local search: n={best_a.size}, {tloc3:.0f}s ===")
        a_out, _ = local_search(
            best_a,
            tloc3,
            seed=505,
            P=80,
            max_pairs=10,
            fracs=(0.04, 0.02, 0.012, 0.006, 0.003),
            verbose=True,
        )
        if a_out is not None:
            s = quick_score(a_out)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_fast:
                best_fast = s
                best_a = a_out.copy()

    # Phase 4: try shifts of final best.
    rem = remaining_time()
    if rem > 20 and best_a is not None:
        best_a, best_fast = try_shifts(best_a, max_shifts=48)
        print(f"After shifts: fast={best_fast:.8f} n={best_a.size}")

    # Final actual-evaluated selection among a few strong candidates.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_a is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
        final_candidates.append(parent)
    for cand in top_candidates[:2]:
        final_candidates.append(cand)

    final_a = best_a if best_a is not None else norm_sum1(np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64))
    final_actual = safe_evaluate(final_a)

    for cand in final_candidates:
        try:
            cand = norm_sum1(cand)
            if cand is None or cand.size == 0 or cand.size > 20000:
                continue
            act = safe_evaluate(cand)
            if np.isfinite(act) and (not np.isfinite(final_actual) or act < final_actual - 1e-12):
                final_actual = act
                final_a = cand.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_a = norm_sum1(final_a)
    final_a = np.maximum(final_a, 0.0)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]

    # One last safety validation. If evaluator disagrees badly or fails,
    # return the normalized sequence anyway; it is a valid nonnegative list.
    try:
        val = safe_evaluate(final_list)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        if np.isfinite(val):
            print(f"\nFINAL validated score: {val:.8f} n={len(final_list)} time={elapsed:.0f}s")
        else:
            print(f"\nWARNING: final safe_evaluate returned inf; returning normalized sequence. time={elapsed:.0f}s")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_list
```
